    // Assembly fallback: pop rbx
    return;
}
    // Assembly fallback: nop dword ptr [rax]
    // Assembly fallback: insb byte ptr [rdi], dx
    // Assembly fallback: xchg esp, eax
    byte ptr [rax], al += ...; // Addition operation
    // Assembly fallback: movabs dword ptr [0x1a000095bb000094], eax
    // Assembly fallback: xchg esi, eax
    byte ptr [rax], al += ...; // Addition operation
